enum cppi5\+\_\+tr\+\_\+event\+\_\+size -\/ TR Flags E\+V\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+S\+I\+ZE field specifies when an event is generated for each TR. @\+C\+P\+P\+I5\+\_\+\+T\+R\+\_\+\+E\+V\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+S\+I\+Z\+E\+\_\+\+C\+O\+M\+P\+L\+E\+T\+I\+ON\+: When TR is complete and all status for the TR has been received @\+C\+P\+P\+I5\+\_\+\+T\+R\+\_\+\+E\+V\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+S\+I\+Z\+E\+\_\+\+I\+C\+N\+T1\+\_\+\+D\+EC\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolType-p}{Type}} 0\+: when the last data transaction is sent for the TR \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolType-p}{Type}} 1-\/11\+: when I\+C\+N\+T1 is decremented @\+C\+P\+P\+I5\+\_\+\+T\+R\+\_\+\+E\+V\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+S\+I\+Z\+E\+\_\+\+I\+C\+N\+T2\+\_\+\+D\+EC\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolType-p}{Type}} 0-\/1,10-\/11\+: when the last data transaction is sent for the TR All other types\+: when I\+C\+N\+T2 is decremented @\+C\+P\+P\+I5\+\_\+\+T\+R\+\_\+\+E\+V\+E\+N\+T\+\_\+\+S\+I\+Z\+E\+\_\+\+I\+C\+N\+T3\+\_\+\+D\+EC\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolType-p}{Type}} 0-\/2,10-\/11\+: when the last data transaction is sent for the TR All other types\+: when I\+C\+N\+T3 is decremented \begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}
